Run DBSCAN\n        db = DBSCAN(eps=epsilon, min_samples=min_samples, metric='precomputed')\n        labels = db.fit_predict(dist_matrix)\n\n        unique_labels = set(labels)\n        unique_labels.discard(-1)\n\n        if len(unique_labels) == 0:\n            f1_scores.append(0.0)\n            deltas.append(np.nan)\n            continue\n\n        # Compute centroids\n        centroids = []\n        for label in unique_labels:\n            cluster_points = points[labels == label]\n            centroid = cluster_points.mean(axis=0)\n            centroids.append(centroid)\n\n        centroids = np.array(centroids)\n\n        # Greedy matching\n        n_centroids = len(centroids)\n        n_experts = len(expert_pts)\n\n        all_dists = []\n        for i in range(n_centroids):\n            for j in range(n_experts):\n                d = np.sqrt((centroids[i, 0] - expert_pts[j, 0])**2 +\n                           (centroids[i, 1] - expert_pts[j, 1])**2)\n                if d <= 100:\n                    all_dists.append((d, i, j))\n\n        all_dists.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\n\n        matched_c = set()\n        matched_e = set()\n        match_dists = []\n\n        for d, i, j in all_dists:\n            if i not in matched_c and j not in matched_e:\n                matched_c.add(i)\n                matched_e.add(j)\n                match_dists.append(d)\n\n        num_matches = len(match_dists)\n\n        if num_matches == 0:\n            f1_scores.append(0.0)\n            deltas.append(np.nan)\n            continue\n\n        tp = num_matches\n        fp = n_centroids - num_matches\n        fn = n_experts - num_matches\n\n        precision = tp / (tp + fp) if (tp + fp) > 0 else 0\n        recall = tp / (tp + fn) if (tp + fn) > 0 else 0\n        f1 = 2 * precision * recall / (precision + recall) if (precision + recall) > 0 else 0\n        delta = np.mean(match_dists)\n\n        f1_scores.append(f1)\n        deltas.append(delta)\n\n    avg_f1 = np.mean(f1_scores)\n    valid_deltas = [d for d in deltas if not np.isnan(d)]\n    avg_delta = np.mean(valid_deltas) if valid_deltas else np.nan\n\n    return (min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight, avg_f1, avg_delta)\n\n\ndef find_pareto_frontier(results_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find Pareto-optimal points: maximize F1, minimize delta.\n    A point is Pareto-optimal if no other point has both higher F1 AND lower delta.\n    \"\"\"\n    pareto_mask = np.ones(len(results_df), dtype=bool)\n\n    for i in range(len(results_df)):\n        for j in range(len(results_df)):\n            if i != j:\n                # Check if j dominates i\n                # j dominates i if j.F1 >= i.F1 AND j.delta <= i.delta\n                # with at least one strict inequality\n                if (results_df.iloc[j]['F1'] >= results_df.iloc[i]['F1'] and\n                    results_df.iloc[j]['delta'] <= results_df.iloc[i]['delta']):\n                    if (results_df.iloc[j]['F1'] > results_df.iloc[i]['F1'] or\n                        results_df.iloc[j]['delta'] < results_df.iloc[i]['delta']):\n                        pareto_mask[i] = False\n                        break\n\n    return results_df[pareto_mask]\n\n\ndef main():\n    # Load data\n    print(\"Loading data...\")\n    citsci_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/citsci_train.csv')\n    expert_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/expert_train.csv')\n\n    print(f\"Citizen science annotations: {len(citsci_df)}\")\n    print(f\"Expert annotations: {len(expert_df)}\")\n\n    # Get unique images from expert dataset\n    all_expert_images = expert_df['file_rad'].unique().tolist()\n    print(f\"Unique expert images: {len(all_expert_images)}\")\n\n    # Pre-group data by file_rad for efficiency\n    citsci_dict = {}\n    for file_rad, group in citsci_df.groupby('file_rad'):\n        citsci_dict[file_rad] = group[['x', 'y']].values\n\n    expert_dict = {}\n    for file_rad, group in expert_df.groupby('file_rad'):\n        expert_dict[file_rad] = group[['x', 'y']].values\n\n    # Define hyperparameter grid\n    min_samples_range = list(range(3, 10))  # 3-9\n    epsilon_range = list(range(4, 25, 2))   # 4, 6, 8, ..., 24\n    shape_weight_range = [round(0.9 + 0.1 * i, 1) for i in range(11)]  # 0.9 to 1.9\n\n    print(f\"min_samples: {min_samples_range}\")\n    print(f\"epsilon: {epsilon_range}\")\n    print(f\"shape_weight: {shape_weight_range}\")\n\n    all_combinations = list(product(min_samples_range, epsilon_range, shape_weight_range))\n    print(f\"Total combinations: {len(all_combinations)}\")\n\n    # Prepare arguments for parallel processing\n    args_list = [\n        (ms, eps, sw, citsci_dict, expert_dict, all_expert_images)\n        for ms, eps, sw in all_combinations\n    ]\n\n    # Run parallel processing\n    print(\"Running grid search...\")\n    n_workers = cpu_count()\n    print(f\"Using {n_workers} workers\")\n\n    with Pool(n_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(worker_func, args_list)\n\n    # Convert results to DataFrame\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame(results, columns=['min_samples', 'epsilon', 'shape_weight', 'F1', 'delta'])\n\n    print(f\"Total results: {len(results_df)}\")\n    print(f\"Results with valid delta: {results_df['delta'].notna().sum()}\")\n\n    # Filter by F1 > 0.5\n    filtered_df = results_df[results_df['F1'] > 0.5].copy()\n    print(f\"Results with F1 > 0.5: {len(filtered_df)}\")\n\n    if len(filtered_df) == 0:\n        print(\"No results with F1 > 0.5. Checking best F1 scores...\")\n        print(results_df.nlargest(10, 'F1')[['min_samples', 'epsilon', 'shape_weight', 'F1', 'delta']])\n        return\n\n    # Remove rows with NaN delta for Pareto analysis\n    filtered_df = filtered_df.dropna(subset=['delta'])\n    print(f\"Results with F1 > 0.5 and valid delta: {len(filtered_df)}\")\n\n    if len(filtered_df) == 0:\n        print(\"No valid results after filtering.\")\n        return\n\n    # Find Pareto frontier\n    print(\"Finding Pareto frontier...\")\n    pareto_df = find_pareto_frontier(filtered_df)\n    print(f\"Pareto-optimal points: {len(pareto_df)}\")\n\n    # Format output\n    output_df = pareto_df[['F1', 'delta', 'min_samples', 'epsilon', 'shape_weight']].copy()\n    output_df['F1'] = output_df['F1'].round(5)\n    output_df['delta'] = output_df['delta'].round(5)\n    output_df['shape_weight'] = output_df['shape_weight'].round(1)\n    output_df['min_samples'] = output_df['min_samples'].astype(int)\n    output_df['epsilon'] = output_df['epsilon'].astype(int)\n\n    # Sort by F1 descending for readability\n    output_df = output_df.sort_values('F1', ascending=False)\n\n    # Save to CSV\n    output_df.to_csv('/root/pareto_frontier.csv', index=False)\n    print(\"\\nPareto frontier saved to /root/pareto_frontier.csv\")\n    print(output_df)\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
